### INT4 Absmax Quantization: One Outlier Zeroed 4,095 of 4,096 Values

![INT4 Absmax Quantization: One Outlier Zeroed 4,095 of 4,096 Values](<https://media2.dev.to/dynamic/image/width=1200,height=627,fit=cover,gravity=auto,format=auto/https%3A%2F%2Fdev-to-uploads.s3.us-east-2.amazonaws.com%2Fuploads%2Farticles%2Fj69ayi6dml95mj9l8885.png>)

### Fuvest antecipa lista de convocados para 2ª fase e locais de prova

### Run your coding agents in tmux and let a local model tell you which replies to read

### Indian bank statement PDF to Excel: handling Cr/Dr, lakh commas and DD-MM-YYYY dates
